# Forward-holdout evaluation — recipeC

- Frame: 2026-05-02 to 2026-11-16 (1,117,257 rows)
- Cutoff: 2026-10-19 (train = 171 days on or before; test = 4 weeks after)
- Test days (28): 2026-10-20, 2026-10-21, 2026-10-22, 2026-10-23, 2026-10-24, 2026-10-25, 2026-10-26, 2026-10-27, 2026-10-28, 2026-10-29, 2026-10-30, 2026-10-31, 2026-11-01, 2026-11-02, 2026-11-03, 2026-11-04, 2026-11-05, 2026-11-06, 2026-11-07, 2026-11-08, 2026-11-09, 2026-11-10, 2026-11-11, 2026-11-12, 2026-11-13, 2026-11-14, 2026-11-15, 2026-11-16
- Git: `926a3bc`
- Recipe B trajectory (delta target, 21 features): `{'n_estimators': 600, 'num_leaves': 63, 'learning_rate': 0.05, 'objective': 'regression_l1', 'verbose': -1}`
- Recipe B day-profile (17 features): `{'n_estimators': 600, 'num_leaves': 63, 'learning_rate': 0.05, 'objective': 'regression_l1', 'verbose': -1}`
- Compared with C trajectory (delta target, 23 features): `{'n_estimators': 600, 'num_leaves': 63, 'learning_rate': 0.05, 'objective': 'regression_l1', 'verbose': -1}`
- Compared with C day-profile (17 features): `{'n_estimators': 600, 'num_leaves': 63, 'learning_rate': 0.05, 'objective': 'regression_l1', 'verbose': -1}`
- Deployed models: working tree models/, trained through 2026-10-25 (recipe C) -> **CONTAMINATED**
  - CONTAMINATED: the deployed models saw some test days; their numbers are shown for reference and excluded from any verdict.
- Unseen rides in test window: none

## Recipe comparison: C (delta) vs B (delta)

## Trend word: regression vs classifier

### T+30 (recipe B)

Same rows. Regression = pred - wait >= +5 / <= -5; classifier = recipes.derive_trend on p_rise_30 / p_fall_30 with the recipe's per-band thresholds (trough counts as rising, peak as falling).

|         |   ('regression', 'recall') |   ('regression', 'precision') |   ('regression', 'support') |   ('classifier', 'recall') |   ('classifier', 'precision') |   ('classifier', 'support') |

| falling |                       0.26 |                          0.72 |                    20209.00 |                       0.29 |                          0.72 |                    20209.00 |

## Known issues in the data this run measured

- Midnight close is encoded as 0 in minutes_until_close / total_operating_hours (data bug, Session 2).
- Ticketed-event rows (Oogie Boogie Bash, Grad Nite) are present after public close.
- crowd_level_ratio baselines are keyed (day_type, hour) resort-wide since Session 3; per-park keying measured as noise overall but helps DCA 18:00-21:00 (reports/eval/2026-10-16_session3-variants.md).
- OPERATING rows with a posted wait of 0 on walkthrough rides are included as-is.
